########################################
# It is done!
########################################
event = {
	id = 2002004
	random = no
	country = VIC
	style = 2
	picture = "Petain"
	save_date = yes

	trigger = {
		war = { country = GER } 
		lost_national = { country = VIC value = 5 } 
		control = { province = 56 data = -2 } #paris lost
		OR = { 
			region = { region = "France" country = -2 data = 1 value = 50 } 
			#Army destroyed
			NOT = { land_percentage = { country = GER value = 0.4 } }
			#Significant army group encircled at the maginot line
			AND = { 
				lost_national = { country = VIC value = 20 } 
				control = { province = 3172	data = -2 } 
				control = { province = 841	data = -2 } 
				control = { province = 317	data = -2 } 
				control = { province = 1643	data = -2 } 
				control = { province = 73	data = -3 } 
				garrison = { province = 73 	country = -3 type = land size = 25 area = yes }
			}
        }
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 6
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	name = "4050name"
	desc = "4050desc"

	action = {
		name = "evt_4050a"
		command = { trigger = { control = { province = 59 data = VIC } } type = capital which = 59 } # Bordeaux - France +
		#command = { type = dissent value = 10 }
		command = { type = trigger which = 2003001 }
	} 
}

event = {
	id = 2003001
	random = no
	country = GER
	style = 2

	name = "4052name" # France enquires about the conditions of armistice
	desc = "4053desc" # After the loss of Paris, the British offer an union to the French government. Before delivering his answer, he like to enquire about the conditions of armistice demanded by Germany. If you demand merciful conditions of armistice, the Frenchmen will be more disposed to collaborate and to refuse British approaches. If contrariwise you demand very hard conditions of armistice, they will have tendency to get closer to Britain. History is yours to make..
	picture = "Petain"

	action = {
        ai_chance = 100
		name = "4053b" # Let us claim a historical partition
		command = { type = setflag which = Rethondes2 } # North coast + Paris + Loire + Atlantic Coast
    } 
	action = {
        ai_chance = 0
		name = "4053a" # Let us claim Paris and the north
		command = { type = setflag which = Rethondes1 } # North coast + Paris
		command = { type = dissent value = 2 } #a bit too lenient
    } 
}

event = {
	id = 2002006
	random = no
	country = VIC
	style = 2

	name = EVT_2002006_NAME
	desc = EVT_2002006_DESC
	picture = "Petain"

	trigger = { 
		event = 2003001 # France enquires about the conditions of armistice
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 3
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = { 
        trigger = { flag = Rethondes1 }
        ai_chance = 100
		name = EVT_2002006_ACTA
		command = { type = secederegion which = -1 value = "France" when = 1 } 
		command = { type = dissent value = 5 }
		
		command = { type = leave_alliance when = 1 }
		command = { type = peace which = GER value = 1 }
		command = { type = access which = GER }
		command = { type = access which = ENG }
		command = { trigger = { alliance = { country = GER country = ITA } } type = access which = ITA }
		command = { trigger = { alliance = { country = GER country = SPA } } type = access which = SPA }
		
	    command = { type = secederegion which = GER value = "Benelux" when = 1 }
	    command = { type = secederegion which = GER value = "Germany" when = 1 }	
		
		command = { type = event which = 2400009 where = VIC when = 0 } #frontLeader no
		command = { type = event which = 2401502 where = VIC when = 0 } #noExpGermany
		command = { type = event which = 2400040 where = VIC when = 0 } #Exp_0
		command = { type = event which = 2002007 where = GER when = 2 } #The armistice of Rethondes
    } 
	action = {
        trigger = { flag = Rethondes1 }
        ai_chance = 0
		name = "4054c" # France fights on
		command = { type = dissent value = 35 }
		command = { type = capital which = 949 } # Alger - Algeria
        command = { type = secedeprovince which = GER value = 72 when = 2 } # Strasbourg - France
        command = { type = secedeprovince which = GER value = 73 when = 2 } # Metz - France
        command = { type = secedeprovince which = GER value = 316 when = 2 } # Colmar - France
		command = { type = trigger which = 2002020 } #French Fleet Seized

        command = { type = remove_division which = 13500 value = 133 } #Paris - Battleship 3
        command = { type = remove_division which = 13500 value = 136 } #Pluton - Light Cruiser 4
        command = { type = remove_division which = 13500 value = 137 } #Jeanne d'Arc - Light Cruiser 4
		command = { type = remove_division which = 13500 value = 139 } #4ème Flottille de Contre-Torpilleurs - Destroyer 1
		command = { type = remove_division which = 13500 value = 138 } #5ème Flottille de Contre-Torpilleurs - Destroyer 1
		command = { type = remove_division which = 13500 value = 150 } #1ère Flottille de Sous-Marins - Submarine 6
		command = { type = remove_division which = 13500 value = 151 } #2ème Flottille de Sous-Marins - Submarine 6

		command = { type = remove_division which = 13500 value = 115 } #2ème Flottille de Convoi - Transport 2
		
		command = { type = clrflag which = Rethondes1 }
    }
	action = {
        trigger = { flag = Rethondes2 }
        ai_chance = 99
		name = EVT_2002006_ACTB

		command = { type = leave_alliance when = 1 }

		command = { type = access which = GER }

		command = { trigger = { alliance = { country = GER country = ITA } } type = access which = ITA }

		command = { type = event which = 2400040 where = VIC when = 0 } #Exp_0

    } 
	action = {
        trigger = { flag = Rethondes2 }
        ai_chance = 1
		name = "4054c" # France fights on
		command = { type = dissent value = 30 }
		command = { type = capital which = 949 } # Alger - Algeria +
        command = { type = secedeprovince which = GER value = 72 when = 2 } # Strasbourg - France
        command = { type = secedeprovince which = GER value = 73 when = 2 } # Metz - France
        command = { type = secedeprovince which = GER value = 316 when = 2 } # Colmar - France

event = {
	id = 2002007
	random = no
	country = GER
	style = 2

	name = "4055name"
	desc = "4055desc"
	picture = "Petain"

	action = { trigger = { flag = Rethondes1 }
		name = ACTION_NAME_OK
		command = { type = dissent value = -10 }
		command = { type = dissent value = 2   }
		
		#possible problem: A puppet will ALWAYS agree to grant military access, if requested. In reality, Vichy was very iffy about letting Germans use their territory except in special cases.

event = {
	id = 2002020
	random = no
	country = ITA
	style = 2

	name = "EVT_2002020_NAME" #French Fleet Seized
	desc = "EVT_2002020_DESC" 

	action = {
		name = "ACTION_NAME_GREAT" #Great
		command = { type = dissent value = -1 }
		command = { type = add_division which = "Courbet"		value = battleship 		when = 2 }
		command = { type = add_division which = "Paris"			value = battleship 		when = 2 }
		command = { type = add_division which = "Pluton"		value = light_cruiser 	when = 5 }
		command = { type = add_division which = "Jeanne d'Arc"	value = light_cruiser 	when = 4 }
		command = { type = add_division 						value = destroyer 		when = 5 }
		command = { type = add_division 						value = destroyer 		when = 5 }
		command = { type = add_division 						value = submarine 		when = 5 }
		command = { type = add_division							value = submarine 		when = 5 }
		command = { type = add_division							value = transport 		when = 2 }
		command = { type = add_division							value = transport 		when = 2 }
    } 
}

event = {
	id = 2002021
	random = no
	country = ENG
	style = 2

	name = EVT_2002021_NAME
	desc = EVT_2002021_DESC

	action = {
		name = EVT_2002021_ACTA
		ai_chance = 100
		trigger = {
			exists = U56
			NOT = { war = { country = ENG country = U56 } }
			NOT = { war = { country = GER country = U56 } }
		}
		command = { type = dissent value = 0.1 }
		command = { type = belligerence value = -0.5 }
		command = { trigger = { control = { province = 7131 data = -3 } } type = secedeprovince which = U56 value = 7131 				when = 2 } 
		command = { trigger = { control = { province = 1720 data = -3 } } type = secedeprovince which = U56 value = 1720 				when = 2 } 
		
		command = { type = event which = 2002022 where = GER when = 1 }
    } 
	action = {
		name = EVT_2002021_ACTB
		ai_chance = 0
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				control = { province = 7131 data = -3 }
				control = { province = 1720 data = -3 }
			}
		}
		command = { type = event which = 2002023 where = ENG when = 30 }
    } 
}

##########################
# L'Etat Français
##########################

event = {
	id = 2002008
	random = no
	country = VIC
	style = 2
	save_date = yes

	name = "4057name" # L'Etat Français
	desc = "4057desc" # Field marshal Pétain uses the French defeat to take power. It flings a final blow to the Republic and sets up a strict regime, the French State. The emergence of such regime opens new geo-strategical opportunities for Germany.
	picture = "Petain"

	action = {
		name = "2002008text"
        #move capital to Vichy
        command = { type = capital which = 360 } # Vichy - France +
		#Disaster... There is some kind of hardcoded bullshit which causes expeditionary AI to become completely retarded if FRA is a puppet of GER but not allied. It works fine if FRA is changed to any other tag though.
		#Possible solutions... 
		#1. Change_tag FRA to VIC. Have to rewrite all the events, and "type=country" will crash in MP so need to have some alternative which is even less than ideal.
		#2. Drop the whole "puppet" thing entirely. May be the best option for the forseable future, but it will look a bit weird that Petain is not a puppet, but De Gaulle is.
		#3. Make FRA both puppet and allied, BUT write an event which regularly checks FRA territory for the presense of Axis troops/navy/air. Very performance heavy and also not foolproof. Subs can stay in ports only briefly when rebasing for example.
		#4. Completely replace FRA with VIC from the very beginning?
		#command = { type = country 		which = VIC   } 
		command = { type = local_setflag 	which = Vichy }
		
		#At the beginning of 1942, the numbers of the Armistice Army were at 550,000 men, including 21,000 officers.
		#In addition, the size of the Gendarmerie was fixed at 60,000 men plus an anti-aircraft force of 10,000 men.
		#However, these numbers include the Levant Army and Indochina army. Approximate distribution
		#Metropolitain France: 160,000 men (100k army, 60k militia)
		#North Africa: 143,000 men (127k army, 16k militia)
		#West Africa: 40,000 men (mostly militia)
		#Levant: 45,000 men (15k army, 30k militia)
		#Indochina: 60,000 men (20k army, 40k militia)
		#Madagascar: 10,000 men (militia)
		command = { type = demobilize which = armor 			when = 0 where = 0 value = 2  }
		command = { type = demobilize which = light_armor 		when = 0 where = 0 value = 2  }
		command = { type = demobilize which = garrison	 		when = 0 where = 0 value = 3  }
		command = { type = demobilize which = hq		 		when = 0 where = 0 value = 3  }
		command = { type = demobilize which = cavalry	 		when = 0 where = 0 value = 3  }
		command = { type = demobilize which = motorized 		when = 0 where = 0 value = 5  }
		command = { type = demobilize which = bergsjaeger 		when = 0 where = 0 value = 5  }
		command = { type = demobilize which = infantry	 		when = 0 where = 0 value = 25 }
		
		command = { trigger = { army = { size = 50 } } type = demobilize which = land when = 0 where = 0 value = 55 } #leave ~55 land units (best models)
		
		#French had only 612 combat planes left at the end of the campaign, which germany reluctantly allowed them to keep. Part of the air force is with U04
		command = { type = demobilize which = strategic_bomber	when = 0 where = 0 value = 0  }
		command = { type = demobilize which = tactical_bomber	when = 0 where = 0 value = 4  }
		command = { type = demobilize which = air	 			when = 0 where = 0 value = 8  }
		
		command = { type = local_setflag which = FRA_AIR when = 1 value = 5 }
		command = { type = event which = 975 where = GER when = 0 }
		command = { type = event which = 975 where = GER when = 0 }
		command = { type = event which = 975 where = GER when = 0 }
		command = { type = event which = 975 where = GER when = 0 }
		command = { type = event which = 975 where = GER when = 0 }
		
		
		#Forced remove all Mobilization.
		command = { type = event which = 8300111 where = VIC when = 0 } 
		command = { type = local_setflag which = demilitarized }
		
		
		command = { type = secederegion which = ITA value = "Ethiopia" when = 1 } 
		
		command = { type = capital which = 360 } # Vichy
		#command = { type = leave_alliance when = 1 }
		#command = { type = peace which = ENG value = 1 }
		#command = { type = peace which = SOV value = 1 }
		command = { type = end_trades which = VIC where = ENG }

event = {
	id = 2001003
	random = no
	country = ENG
	style = 2
	picture = "Appel_de_Gaulle"
	decision_picture = "decision_appel_de_gaulle"

	name = "4060name"
	desc = "4060desc"

	decision = { 
		NOT = { 
			exists = U01 
			region = { region = France country = -3 data = 2 value = 1 } 
		}
		war =  { country = ENG country = GER }
	}
	decision_trigger = { 
		event = { id = 2002008 days = 10 } #L'Etat Français
		NOT = { exists = U01 }
		war =  { country = ENG country = GER }
	}
	trigger = { 
		event = { id = 2002008 days = 10 } #L'Etat Français
		NOT = {
			exists = U01 
			region = { region = France country = -3 data = 2 value = 1 } 
		}
		war =  { country = ENG country = GER }
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 15
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		name = "4060a"
		command = { type = relation which = VIC value = -100 }
		
		command = { type = remove_units	which = all where = -2 when = 1 value = "Engels" }
		
        command = { type = secedeprovince which = ENG value = 1075 when = 2 } # Brazzaville - Central Africa +
        command = { type = secedeprovince which = ENG value = 2750 when = 2 } # Pointe-Noire - Central Africa +
		command = { type = secedeprovince which = ENG value = 4890 when = 2 }
		command = { type = secedeprovince which = ENG value = 4889 when = 2 }
		command = { type = secedeprovince which = ENG value = 7339 when = 2 }
		command = { type = secedeprovince which = ENG value = 4891 when = 2 }
		
		command = { type = independence which = U01 value = 1 }
        #command = { type = alliance which = U01 when = 1 }
		command = { type = make_puppet which = U01 }
		command = { type = free_ic  value = -5 }
		command = { type = event which = 2400539 where = U01 when = 0  } #strong puppet 
        command = { type = event which = 2002009 where = VIC when = 5  }
		command = { type = event which = 2001004 where = ENG when = 15 } #attack on Mers-el-Kebir
    } 
}

event = {
	id = 2002009
	random = no
	country = VIC
	style = 2
	picture = "news_paper"

	name = EVT_2002009_NAME
	desc = "4061desc"

	action = {
		name = "4061c"
		command = { type = secederegion which = U01 value = "Central Africa" 		when = 1 }
		command = { type = secederegion which = U01 value = "South Solomon Islands" when = 1 }
		command = { type = secederegion which = U01 value = "American Pacific" 		when = 1 }
		command = { type = secederegion which = U01 value = "India"			 		when = 1 }
		command = { type = secedeprovince which = VIC value = 1752 when = 2 } #Wallis and Futuna stayed with Vichy until ~mid 1942
		
		#command = { type = demobilize which = infantry when = 0 where = 1 value = -1 } #PROBLEMS
		
		command = { type = dissent value = 2 }
		
		command = { type = event which = 2002012 where = U01 when = 0 }
		command = { type = event which = 2001007 where = GER when = 1 }
    } 
}

event = {
	id = 2002012
	random = no
	country = U01
	style = 2
	picture = "news_paper"

	name = EVT_2002009_NAME
	desc = "4061desc"

	action = {
		name = "4061c"
		command = { type = free_ic value = 5 }
		command = { type = trade which = ENG where = U01 supplies = -5 value = 1 } 
		
		command = { type = local_setflag which = WARDebt when = 0 value = 12 }
		
		command = { type = transport_pool which = U01 value = 150 }
		
		command = { type = local_setflag which = sliderSet	}
		command = { type = local_setflag which = exiled		}
		command = { type = set_domestic which = political_left 	value = 5 } #ML
		command = { type = set_domestic which = interventionism value = 3 }
		command = { type = set_domestic which = freedom 		value = 9 }
		command = { type = set_domestic which = free_market 	value = 6 }
		command = { type = manpowerpool value = 300 } #defections and new recruits
		
		command = { type = headofstate 				which = 820004 } #Gaulle (SC)
		command = { type = headofgovernment 		which = 820010 } #Gaulle (SC)
		command = { type = foreignminister 			which = 820069 } #Dejean (SL)
		command = { type = ministerofintelligence 	which = 820095 } #Soustelle (ML)
		command = { type = ministerofsecurity		which = 820094 } #Diethelm (SC)
		command = { type = armamentminister 		which = 820027 } #Pleven (ML)
		command = { type = chiefofstaff 			which = 820054 } #Legentilhomme (SC)
		command = { type = chiefofarmy 				which = 820092 } #Gaulle (SC)
		command = { type = chiefofnavy 				which = 820091 } #Muselier (SC)
		command = { type = chiefofair 				which = 820066 } #Valin (ML)
		
		command = { type = add_corps which = "Troupes d'Afrique Centrale" value = land where = 1075 }  #+
		command = { type = add_division which = "Equatorial Africa garrison" value = garrison when = 4 }
		command = { type = add_division which = "French Forces in Africa" value = infantry when = 10 }
		
		command = { type = add_corps value = land where = 1692 }  # New Caledonia +
		command = { type = add_division which = "New Caledonia garrison" value = garrison when = 4 }
		
		command = { type = add_corps which = "Forces Aeriennes Francaises Libres" value = land where = 1075 } 
		command = { type = add_division which = "Groupe de Chasse 1 'Alsace'" value = interceptor when = 8 where = -80 }
		
		command = { type = supplies value = 2000 where = 1692 }
		command = { type = oilpool value = 200 where = 1692 }
		
		command = { type = event which = 2002015 where = U01 when = 5 }
    } 
}

event = {
	id = 2002000
	random = no
	country = VIC
	style = 2
	picture = "news_paper"

	name = "4067name"
	desc = "4067desc"
	
	trigger = { 
		OR = {
			war = { country = ENG }
			war = { country = U01 }
			puppet = { country = VIC country = GER }
		}
		event = { id = 2002008 days = 30 }
		OR = {
			alliance = { country = GER }
			AND = {
				NOT = { region = { region = "Algeria"	country = -3 data = 1 value = 70 } }
				NOT = { region = { region = "Morocco"	country = -3 data = 1 value = 50 } }
			}
			AND = {
				event = { id = 2002008 days = 180 }
				OR = {
					AND = {
						ai = no
						NOT = { garrison = { country = VIC province = 4453 type = land size = 6 area = 2 } } #West Africa area
					}
					NOT = { garrison = { country = VIC province = 4453 type = land size = 3 area = 2 } }
				}
				random = 15
			}
			local_flag = VichyCoupFailed
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 5
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		name = ACTION_NAME_DAMN
		command = { type = remove_units which = all where = REB when = 2 value = "West Africa" }
		
		command = { type = independence which = EAF value = 0 } #stationed units will change sides
		command = { type = event which = 2400530 where = EAF when = 0 } #legit indie
		command = { type = event which = 2002025 where = EAF when = 0 }
		
		#command = { type = remove_units which = all where = VIC when = 2 value = "West Africa" }
		command = { type = remove_units which = all where = VIC when = 2 value = "Central Africa" }
		command = { type = remove_units which = all where = VIC when = 2 value = "Horn of Africa" }
		command = { type = remove_units which = all where = VIC when = 2 value = "South Solomon Islands" }
		command = { type = remove_units which = all where = VIC when = 2 value = "American Pacific" }
		command = { type = remove_units which = all where = VIC when = 2 value = "India" }
		
		command = { trigger = { control = { province = 1134 data = -3 } } type = independence which = MAD value = 0 } # stationed units will change sides

		command = { type = secedeprovince which = MAD value = 1134 when = 2 }
		command = { type = secedeprovince which = MAD value = 1137 when = 2 }
		command = { type = secedeprovince which = MAD value = 2819 when = 2 }
		command = { type = secedeprovince which = MAD value = 2818 when = 2 }
		command = { type = secedeprovince which = MAD value = 4901 when = 2 }

		command = { type = secedeprovince which = MAD value = 4903 when = 2 }
		command = { type = secedeprovince which = MAD value = 4904 when = 2 }
		command = { type = secedeprovince which = MAD value = 4905 when = 2 }
		command = { type = secedeprovince which = MAD value = 4906 when = 2 }
		command = { type = secedeprovince which = MAD value = 4907 when = 2 }
		command = { type = secedeprovince which = MAD value = 4908 when = 2 }
		command = { type = secedeprovince which = MAD value = 4909 when = 2 }
		command = { type = secedeprovince which = MAD value = 4910 when = 2 }
		command = { type = secedeprovince which = MAD value = 4911 when = 2 }

		command = { type = secedeprovince which = VIC value = 5094 when = 2 }
		command = { type = secedeprovince which = VIC value = 5095 when = 2 }

		command = { type = secedeprovince which = MAD value = 1135 when = 2 } #Reunion island
		command = { type = addcore which = 1135 where = MAD }
		
		command = { type = event which = 2002025 where = MAD when = 0 }
		
		command = { trigger = { event = 2011040 war = { country = JAP country = ENG } } 			type = event which = 985 where = JAP when = 0 } #Japan grabs Indochina/madagascar
		command = { trigger = { event = 2011040 NOT = { war = { country = JAP country = ENG } } } 	type = event which = 985 where = GER when = 0 } #Germany grabs Indochina/madagascar
		
		command = { type = secederegion which = U01 value = "West Africa" 			when = 1 }
		command = { type = secederegion which = U01 value = "Central Africa" 		when = 1 }

		command = { type = secederegion which = ENG value = "American Pacific" 		when = 1 }

event = { 
	id = 2002015
	random = no 
	country = U01
	persistent = yes
	
	name = EVT_2002015_NAME
	desc = EVT_2002015_DESC
  	style = 2
	
	action = {
		name = "ACTION_NAME_GREAT"
		trigger = {
			event = 2001003
			NOT = { event = 2002013 }
			atwar = U01
			NOT = { local_flag = { which = U01ArmyWest when = 1 value = 10 } }
		}
		
		command = { trigger = { control = { province = 1075 data = -3 } } 			type = add_corps value = land where = 1075 } #Brazzaville
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { control = { province = 1075 data = -3 } } }	type = add_corps value = land where = 19 } #London
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { year = 1943 } } 							type = add_division value = infantry when = 10 where = -90 } #1939
		command = { trigger = { year = 1943 }										type = add_division value = infantry when = 11 where = -90 } #1942
		
		command = { type = local_setflag which = U01ArmyWest when = 1 value = 1 }
		
		command = { type = event which = 2002015 where = U01 when = 120 } #One division per 4 months. Will reach 8 divisions by ~May 1943. If U01 turns back into FRA, this chain will end prematurely
	}
}

#Vichy coup if manually unpuppeted

event = { 
	id = 2002018
	random = no 
	#invisible
	country = REB

	trigger = {
		event = 2002008
		NOT = { 
			event = 982 #Vichy changed sides

event = {
	id = 960
	random = no
	tag = { ENG USA }
	style = 2
	picture = "Petain"
	decision_picture = "decision_grand_offensive4"

	decision = {
		war = { country = VIC }
		war = { country = GER }
		NOT = { war = { country = GER country = VIC } }
		#NOT = { alliance = { country = GER country = VIC } }
		#non_aggression = { country = VIC country = ENG }
	}
	decision_trigger = { 
		#OR = { 
		#	control = { province = 348	data = -3 }
		#	control = { province = 932	data = -3 }
		#	control = { province = 31	data = -3 }
		#}
	}
	trigger = {
		ai = USA #AI ENG will only do if USA is also AI
		war = { country = VIC }
		war = { country = GER }
		NOT = { war = { country = GER country = VIC } }
		#NOT = { alliance = { country = GER country = VIC } }
		#non_aggression = { country = VIC country = ENG }
		#NOT = { region = { region = "Egypt" country = -2 value = 5 } }
		
		OR = { 
			control = { province = 348	data = -3 }
			control = { province = 932	data = -3 }
			control = { province = 31	data = -3 }
		}
		OR = {
			AND = {
				alliance = { country = ENG country = USA }
				NOT = { flag = debuff_USA }
			}
			event = { id = 2002011 days = 1080 } #3 years since attack on Mers-el-Kebir - UK goes alone
		}
		OR = {
			region = { region = "Libya" country = -3 value = 30 } #Advancing in Libya
			control = { province = 515 data = -3 } #Took Rome
			AND = {
				event = { id = 2002011 days = 720 } #2 years since attack on Mers-el-Kebir
				alliance = { country = ENG country = USA }
				NOT = { flag = debuff_USA }
			}
		}
		OR = {
			AND = {
				ai = VIC
				NOT = { alliance = { country = GER country = VIC } }
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = { garrison = { country = -2	province = 4392		type = land size = 6  area = 1 } }
				NOT = { garrison = { country = -2	province = 4392		type = land size = 2  area = 0 } }
				NOT = { garrison = { country = -2	province = 2911		type = land size = 6  area = 0 } }
				NOT = { garrison = { country = -2	province = 2911		type = land size = 3  area = 0 } }
				NOT = { garrison = { country = -2	province = 949		type = land size = 6  area = 0 } }
				NOT = { garrison = { country = -2	province = 949		type = land size = 3  area = 0 } }
			}
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	name = EVT_960_NAME
	desc = EVT_960_DESC

	action = {
		name = EVT_960_ACTA
		command = { trigger = { non_aggression = { country = VIC country = ENG } } type = dissent value = 1 } #Not the most popular target... (still better than getting +5 from breaking the NAP manually)
		command = { type = belligerence value = 5   }
		command = { type = trigger 		which = 961 }
		
		command = { trigger = { alliance = { country = ENG country = USA } ai = VIC ai = USA } type = event which = 2405030 where = USA when = 0 }
	}
}

event = {
	id = 984
	random = no
	country = VIC

	name = EVT_984_NAME
	desc = EVT_984_DESC
	style = 2
	picture = "news_paper"

	#decision = {
	#	event = 2002008 # French State
	#}
	trigger = {
		#atwar = no 
		event = 2002008 # French State
		alliance = { country = ENG }
		alliance = { country = U01 }
		OR = {
			NOT = { exists = U01 }
			NOT = { event = 982 } #Vichy folds
			event = { id = 982 days = 180 }

event = {
	id = 2184007
	random = no
	tag = { U01 VIC }
	persistent = yes

	name = "EVT_2184007_NAME" #Move the Capital
	desc = "EVT_2184007_DESC"
	style = 2
	picture = "news_paper"

	decision = {
		NOT = { capital = { province = 56 } } #Paris is not the capital of Free France
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		OR = {
	      	control = { province = 56 } #Free France controls Paris
	      	AND = {
				control = { province = 949 } #Free France controls Algiers
				NOT = { capital = { province = 949 } }
				NOT = { control = { province = 56 } control = { province = 360 } } #Free France does not controls Paris
			}
		} 
	}
	trigger = {
		NOT = { capital = { province = 56 } } #Paris is not the capital of Free France
		OR = {
	      	control = { province = 56 } #Free France controls Paris
	      	AND = {
				control = { province = 949 } #Free France controls Algiers
				NOT = { capital = { province = 949 } }
				NOT = { control = { province = 56 } control = { province = 360 } } #Free France does not controls Paris or Vichy
			}
		} 
	}

	date = { day = 2 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		trigger = { control = { province = 56 } }
		name = "EVT_2184007_ACTIONA" #To Paris!
		command = { type = secedeprovince which = -3 value = 56 when = 2 }
		command = { type = capital which = 56 } # Paris +
		command = { type = dissent value = -3 }
		command = { type = sleepevent which = 2184007 }
	}
	action = {
		trigger = { control = { province = 949 } NOT = { control = { province = 56 } control = { province = 360 } } }
		name = "EVT_2184007_ACTIONB" #To Algiers!
		command = { type = secedeprovince which = -3 value = 949 when = 2 }
